        index_expr = ops.index_expr(get_index, torch.int64)

        lt = ops.lt(index_expr, constant)
        to_dtype = ops.to_dtype(lt, torch.int64, src_dtype = torch.bool)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(16, torch.int64)
        mul = ops.mul(to_dtype, constant_1)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(0, torch.int64)

        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        index_expr_1 = ops.index_expr(get_index_1, torch.int64)
        constant_3 = ops.constant(8, torch.int64)
        lt_1 = ops.lt(index_expr_1, constant_3)

        index_expr_2 = ops.index_expr(get_index_2, torch.float32)

        truediv = ops.truediv(index_expr_2, constant_4)
        log = ops.log(truediv)
        constant_5 = ops.constant(2.772588722239781, torch.float32)
        truediv_1 = ops.truediv(log, constant_5)
        constant_6 = ops.constant(8.0, torch.float32)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(truediv_1, constant_6)
        to_dtype_1 = ops.to_dtype(mul_1, torch.int64, src_dtype = torch.float32)
        constant_7 = ops.constant(8, torch.int64)

        index_expr_3 = ops.index_expr(get_index_3, torch.int64)
        where = ops.where(lt_1, index_expr_3, minimum)

        constant_1 = ops.constant(0, torch.int64)
